# Florida, Texas & Georgia Acute Care Hospitals: CMS General Information Matched to Complications (`xubh-q36u` × `ynj2-r877`), Ranked, Two Pulls

Public sources only; no contact with anyone.

## 1. Rule 25 DATA FILES & Two-Pull PLATFORM RECORD Summary (Acceptance #1 & #6)

- **PULL 1 (`2026-11-03 03:08:28.760641+00`):** `hospital-recon-pull1.csv` — **`733`** rows (excluding header), SHA-256 `72c3d92a78bcabdb46f010debd31c81f1bd896e46c2a94388319f75676aac38b`, Source HTTP `Date` (Request 1): `Tue, 03 Nov 2026 03:06:40 GMT`
- **PULL 2 (`2026-11-04 03:14:33.85517+00`):** `hospital-recon-pull2.csv` — **`733`** rows (excluding header), SHA-256 `72c3d92a78bcabdb46f010debd31c81f1bd896e46c2a94388319f75676aac38b`, Source HTTP `Date` (Request 1): `Wed, 04 Nov 2026 03:13:32 GMT`
- **Exact 13 CSV Columns (in order):** `facility_id, facility_name, state, in_general_information, in_complications, hospital_overall_rating, hospital_overall_rating_footnote, count_of_mort_measures_worse, count_of_safety_measures_worse, count_of_readm_measures_worse, complication_rows, worse_measures, rank`
- **Key & Matching Rule:** `facility_id` preserved strictly as text (retaining leading zeros and alphanumeric VA/DoD suffixes such as `10009F`); 1 row per `facility_id` (`0` duplicate keys). Empty source cells stay empty; `worse_measures` counts only the exact string `'Worse Than the National Rate'`; `rank` (`1, 2, 3...`) is assigned only to matched hospitals (`in_general_information = yes` and `in_complications = yes`) with a numeric `hospital_overall_rating` (`higher rating first`, then `fewer worse_measures`, then `facility_id` ascending as text).

```synmerco-data
72c3d92a78bcabdb46f010debd31c81f1bd896e46c2a94388319f75676aac38b hospital-recon-pull1.csv
72c3d92a78bcabdb46f010debd31c81f1bd896e46c2a94388319f75676aac38b hospital-recon-pull2.csv
```

## 3. Hospitals Present in One Dataset Only — State, Name & How Found (Acceptance #3)

### 3A. In General Information (`xubh-q36u` Acute Care Hospitals) Only, Not in Complications (`ynj2-r877`)

**None** (`0` hospitals) — every single one of the `548` acute care hospitals returned by `xubh-q36u/0` across FL (`167`), TX (`285`), and GA (`96`) is also present in `ynj2-r877/0`.

### 3B. In Complications (`ynj2-r877`) Only, Not in General Information Acute Care (`185` Hospitals)

These facilities (Critical Access Hospitals, VA/DoD Federal hospitals with `F` suffixes, Children's, Psychiatric, or Specialty hospitals) appear in the state-level `ynj2-r877/0` complications pulls for FL, TX, and GA, and were excluded from the `xubh-q36u/0` `hospital_type = Acute Care Hospitals` pull.
